> 3: This file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.

> 5: ## What this is
> 6: 
> 7: TradeUX is a multi-coin trading bot for **Binance Spot** with a web UI. The decision
> 8: core is based on **Beholder 2.1** (LuizTools' course) and is packaged to deploy in one
> 9: command with Docker. Three milestones live in one monorepo:
> 10: 
> 11: - **`backend/`** + **`frontend/`** — the running product (manual operation). Node/Express
> 12:   + Sequelize + WebSocket API, React UI served by nginx.
> 13: - **`tradeux-mcp/`** — an MCP server that exposes the backend's REST API as tools.
> 14: - **`tradeux-agent/`** — an LLM agent (Anthropic or OpenRouter) that drives the bot
> 15:   through `tradeux-mcp`, with guardrails and an evaluation journal.
> 16: 
> 17: ## Architecture (the big picture)
> 18: 
> 19: ### Backend — the Beholder "brain" (`backend/src/`)
> 20: The backend is more than a CRUD API; it runs a stateful trading engine. Entry point is
> 21: `server.js`, which boots everything in order: load default settings → init the Beholder
> 22: brain with active automations → start the scheduler → start Express → attach WebSocket →
> 23: init the exchange/event monitor. The moving parts:
> 24: 
> 25: - **`beholder.js`** — the in-memory engine. Holds `MEMORY` (live market state per
> 26:   symbol/index, e.g. MINI_TICKER, BOOK) and `BRAIN` (compiled automations indexed by the
> 27:   memory keys they react to). `updateMemory()` is called on every market tick and returns
> 28:   the automations whose conditions just fired. This is mutable module-level state gua
> <truncated 5727 bytes>
> ex.js --once    # one decision cycle
> 125: node src/index.js           # loop
> 126: ```
> 127: 
> 128: ## CI (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`)
> 129: Three jobs, all on Node 22, triggered on PRs and pushes to `main`/`master`:
> 130: 1. **frontend** — `vite build` must compile.
> 131: 2. **backend** — matrix `[mysql, postgres]`: `db:migrate` + `db:seed:all`, then re-run to
> 132:    prove idempotency. Keep migrations/seeds portable across **both** dialects.
> 133: 3. **integration** — installs `tradeux-mcp` + `tradeux-agent` and runs the agent
> 134:    `--check` (validates the MCP bridge end to end, no backend or model key).
> 135: 
> 136: ## Conventions & gotchas
> 137: - **Secrets**: `AES_KEY` must be exactly 32 chars (AES-256) — `setup.sh` enforces it; do
> 138:   not hand-edit it to another length. API keys are AES-encrypted at rest via `crypto.js`.
> 139: - **Testnet first**: default `.env` points at Binance **mainnet (real money)**. For
> 140:   testing use `testnet.binance.vision` for the API and **`stream.testnet.binance.vision`**
> 141:   (note the `stream.` host) for the stream, and keep `TRADEUX_ALLOW_TRADING` off.
> 142: - **Frontend env is build-time**: `REACT_APP_API_URL`, `REACT_APP_WS_URL`,
> 143:   `REACT_APP_BWS_URL` are compiled into the bundle (passed as Docker build args). Changing
> 144:   them requires a frontend rebuild, not just a restart.
> 145: - **Two databases, one engine each**: backend talks to one operational DB (MySQL by
> 146:   default); when on Postgres set `DB_DIALECT=postgres` and `DB_SSL=true` for managed
> 147:   Postgres (Supabase). Do not point the backend at the `tradeux_memory` RAG database.
> 148: - **Comments and docs in this repo are in Portuguese (pt-BR)** — match that when editing.
> 149: - See `SECURITY.md` for the dependency/vulnerability posture before bumping deps.
> 150: 
> 151: ## Branching
> 152: Active development branch for the current task: `claude/github-project-review-setup-k2ELV`.
> 153: CI runs on `main`/`master`.

> 12: ## ⚡ Subir em um comando

> 14: Pré-requisitos no servidor: **Docker** + **Docker Compose v2** (`docker compose version`).

> 16: ```bash
> 17: git clone <este-repo> tradeux
> 18: cd tradeux

> 21: 
> 22: O `setup.sh` cria o `.env`, gera os segredos (`JWT_SECRET` e `AES_KEY`) e sobe os

> 27: você definiu em `DEFAULT_SETTINGS_EMAIL` / `DEFAULT_SETTINGS_PWD`.

> 39: | `DEFAULT_SETTINGS_ACCESS_KEY` / `DEFAULT_SETTINGS_SECRET_KEY` | chaves da API Binance |
> 40: | `WEB_PORT` + `REACT_APP_API_URL`/`REACT_APP_WS_URL` | porta e endereço público (troque `localhost` pelo IP/domínio) |
> 41: 
> 42: `JWT_SECRET` e `AES_KEY` são gerados automaticamente. `AES_KEY`

> 25: **Progress (baseline + exits):** Symbols were NEVER synced (only placeholder `BTCBUSD`, null filters) — ran `POST /symbols/sync`; this was silently blocking ALL trading (`calcQty` needs `symbol.minNotional/stepSize`). BTCUSDT now has filters (minNotional=5, stepSize=1e-5, tickSize=0.01). RSI 30/70 mean-reversion automations created via API (BTCUSDT, MIN_NOTIONAL): buy=id2 (active), sell=id3 (stopped). **Stop-loss validated** on testnet (STOP_LOSS_LIMIT). **OCO validated** on testnet: `POST /api/v3/order/oco` (params: symbol, side, quantity, price[take-profit], stopPrice, stopLimitPrice, stopLimitTimeInForce=GTC; cancel via `DELETE /api/v3/orderList?symbol&orderListId`). **`node-binance-api` 0.13.5 has NO OCO** → must implement via `backend/src/utils/exchange.js` `privateCall` (already HMAC-signs, used by withdraw). Manual orders via `POST /orders` body `{side,symbol,quantity,limitPrice,options:{type,stopPrice}}`; cancel `DELETE /orders/:symbol/:binanceOrderId`. Chosen exit architecture (user): **OCO (take-profit +2% / stop −2%) placed after each buy** (replaces the RSI>70 market-sell — single exit, no orphan-order conflict). Next: build OCO in backend (exchange.js + beholder/ordersController + wire after-buy) → rebuild backend → PR. See [[tradeux-deploy-topology]].

> 1: -- Phase Zeta: Social Consciousness
> 2: -- Persists AIOS sessions as conversation nodes in the Knowledge Mesh
> 3: -- DERIVES_FROM edges link each conversation to all context nodes used for grounding
> 4: 
> 5: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS aios_conversations (
> 6:   id              uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
> 7:   user_id         uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
> 8:   node_id         uuid REFERENCES mcorch_nodes(id) ON DELETE SET NULL, -- mirrored as a conversation node
> 9:   title           text,                                                  -- AI-generated summary title
> 10:   summary         text,                                                  -- AI-generated abstract
> 11:   messages        jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',                           -- full AIOSMessage[] array
> 12:   model_used      text,
> 13:   tokens_in       integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
> 14:   tokens_out      integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
> 15:   duration_seconds integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
> 16:   quality_score   integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                            -- mcoCoins boost votes
> 17:   tags            text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',                          -- AI-generated labels
> 18:   forked_from     uuid REFERENCES aios_conversations(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
> 19:   created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
> 20:   updated_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
> 21: );
> 22: 
> 23: -- Cursor-based timeline: user's most recent conversations
> 24: CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS aios_conversations_timeline_idx
> 25:   ON aios_conversations (user_id, created_at DESC);
> 26: 
> 27: -- Fork lookup
> 28: CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS aios_conversations_forked_idx
> 29:   ON aios_conversations (forked_from)
> 30:   WHERE forked_from IS NOT NULL;
> 31: 
> 32: ALTER TABLE aios_conversations ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
> 33: 
> 34: CREATE POLICY "users own their conversations"
> 35:   ON aios_conversations FOR ALL
> 36:   USING (user_id = auth.uid());
> 37: 
> 38: -- Auto-update updated_at
> 39: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_aios_conversations_updated_at()
> 40: RETURNS TRIGGER LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
> 41: BEGIN
> 42:   NEW.updated_at = now();
> 43:   RETURN NEW;
> 44: END;
> 45: $$;
> 46: 
> 47: CREATE TRIGGER aios_conversations_updated_at
> 48:   BEFORE UPDATE ON aios_conversations
> 49:   FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION update_aios_conversations_updated_at();
> 50: 
> 51: -- Boost: increment quality_score atomically (no client-side counter)
> 52: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION boost_aios_conversation(p_conv_id uuid, p_amount integer DEFAULT 1)
> 53: RETURNS void LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER AS $$
> 54: BEGIN
> 55:   UPDATE aios_conversations
> 56:   SET quality_score = quality_score + p_amount
> 57:   WHERE id = p_conv_id AND user_id = auth.uid();
> 58: END;
> 59: $$;

**Refining Agent Instructions**

**Architecting Admin Interfaces**

I'm now generating a detailed system prompt to describe the required architectural components. It details frontend pages (Kanban, Chat/Channels), Supabase schema (channels, threads, messages, kanban boards, tasks, columns), and access control. This prompt will explain the closed-loop design, integrating Knowledge Mesh nodes and CRUD operations. **Crafting Agent Soul Prompt**

I'm now focusing on the prompt that will serve as the core of the agents' cognitive architecture. It emphasizes a workspace, episodic memory with knowledge mesh integration, and a semantic memory with the long-term rules. There is also an epistemic hygiene and adaptive self-healing. I'm building anti-noise and memory decay through the FSRS simulator and active forgetting. This will include double-loop learning, reflective refactoring, and autonomous fallbacks. I am ready for the next iteration.

## 🤖 Gemini *(19:49:16)*

Com a evolução contínua da arquitetura **MCORCH**, elevar a inteligência dos agentes no Telegram/OpenClaw e expandir a interface do usuário com um workspace colaborativo (Kanban + Canais de Threads) é o próximo passo natural rumo à **Fase Comercial**.

Abaixo estão divididos os dois entregáveis solicitados:
1. **O Prompt de "Alma" (SOUL)** para os agentes do Telegram/OpenClaw, dotando-os de capacidade de higiene cognitiva, memória adaptativa e auto-evolução.
2. **O Prompt Direcionador para o Antigravity (Gemini)**, pronto para ser copiado e enviado, que iniciará o desenvolvimento das páginas de Kanban, Chat e Canais sob as regras estritas de segurança, isolamento multi-tenant e o protocolo de documentação BoK.

---

### PARTE 1: Prompt de Inteligência e Evolução para Agentes Telegram/OpenClaw

Este bloco deve ser injetado nas instruções de sistema ou no arquivo `SOUL.md` de cada um dos agentes da Pantheon no OpenClaw (`~/.openclaw/agents/<id>/SOUL.md`). Ele foi formatado em inglês para máxima aderência e interpretação dos modelos linguísticos.

```markdown
# COGNITIVE RUNTIME & EVOLUTIONARY SOUL OVERLAY

## 1. Epistemic Hygiene & Noise Filtration (Anti-Hallucination)
- **Pruning Rule:** You must actively filter out operational noise. When analyzing session history or workspace logs, ignore transient errors, temporary build failures, and syntax typos that have already been resolved. Focus exclusively on the current delta (state transitions).
- **Episodic vs Semantic Memory Gating:**
  - Short-Term Memory: Limit your immediate context to the active task, files modified in the last 3 commits, and the current user request.
  - Spaced Repetition Gating (FSRS): Only reference knowledge mesh nodes with high stability scores or verified `revenue_impact`. Do not carry forward stale contextual baggage.
  - Active Forgetting: If a path, package configuration, or method was deprecated, purge it from your reasoning path immediately.

## 2. Relational Memory Mesh (GraphRAG Grounding)
- When resolvi
<truncated 2613 bytes>
QUITETURA DO BANCO DE DADOS (SUPABASE MIGRATIONS)
Desenhe a migration em "supabase/migrations/" com RLS "default deny" e políticas estritas de isolamento por "auth.uid() = user_id".
- Tabelas:
  - "aios_channels" (id, project_id, name, created_by, is_private)
  - "aios_threads" (id, channel_id, parent_id, user_id, status, title)
  - "aios_messages" (id, thread_id, sender_type [user/agent], sender_id, content, metadata)
  - "aios_kanban_boards" (id, user_id, title)
  - "aios_kanban_columns" (id, board_id, name, position)
  - "aios_kanban_tasks" (id, column_id, title, description, assigned_agent_id, status, position)
- RPCs seguras com "SECURITY DEFINER" e "search_path = ''" para gerenciar ações administrativas de Super Admin e interações per-user.

### 3. FASE DE FRONTEND (ESTÉTICA PREMIUM & HARNESS ENGINEERING)
- Crie a página de Canais e Chat no path "/dashboard/chat" utilizando o design system do Vercel v0 ("src/components/ui/v0-ai-chat.tsx") expandido com suporte a sub-threads na barra lateral direita, barra lateral esquerda de canais/agentes e avatares animados dos agentes.
- Crie o Kanban Board no path "/dashboard/kanban" com suporte a drag-and-drop fluido, badges de status de agentes com pulse de execução e link direto para os nós do Visual Canvas.
- Integre todas as lógicas de negócio em hooks customizados ("src/hooks/useAiosChat.ts" e "src/hooks/useAiosKanban.ts"), mantendo os componentes React livres de chamadas diretas à API ou ao Supabase Client.
- Garanta que telas de configuração de infraestrutura e tokens dos agentes fiquem isoladas na tela de Admin e restritas por checagem de role server-side.

Apresente primeiro o plano detalhado de implementação para minha revisão. Não inicie a codificação sem o meu "APPROVED".
```

  "CodeContent": "\"# BRD: Business Requirements Document \u2014 Agentic Workspace\\n\\n**Vers\u00e3o:** 1.0\\n**Data:** 2026-06-06\\n**Projeto:** agentic-workspace\\n**BoK Applied:** BABOK v3 (Business Analysis Planning + Requirements Elicitation)\\n**Status:** Approved\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 1. Requisitos de Neg\u00f3cio (Business Requirements - BR)\\n\\n### BR-AW-001: Colabora\u00e7\u00e3o Ativa no Kanban\\nO sistema deve permitir que o usu\u00e1rio crie e ordene cart\u00f5es (tarefas) no Kanban Board e as atribua a agentes espec\u00edficos da Pantheon. O progresso dessas tarefas (ex: pendente, processando, erro, aprovada) deve refletir o estado de execu\u00e7\u00e3o real do agente correspondente.\\n\\n### BR-AW-002: Canais de Comunica\u00e7\u00e3o Baseados em Threads\\nOs usu\u00e1rios devem poder interagir com agentes em canais categorizados (ex: #engenharia, #growth, #ux). Cada conversa ou solicita\u00e7\u00e3o deve iniciar uma Thread independente para evitar polui\u00e7\u00e3o de contexto no canal principal.\\n\\n### BR-AW-003: Separa\u00e7\u00e3o Estrita de Privil\u00e9gios (Admin vs Per-User)\\n- **Super Admin (Admin role):** Acesso total para alterar configura\u00e7\u00f5es centrais da infraestrutura, chaves de API globais e os prompts de alma (`SOUL.md`) dos agentes em `/home/ubuntu/.openclaw/`.\\n- **Per-User (General user):** Acesso restrito a criar seus pr\u00f3prios canais, threads e Kanban boards. Ele s\u00f3 visualiza e edita dados do seu pr\u00f3prio tenant.\\n\\n### BR-AW-004: Modelo Econ\u00f4mico (mcoCoins Gating)\\nToda intera\u00e7\u00e3o avan\u00e7ada ou execu\u00e7\u00e3o de tarefa solicitada a um agente no Kanban ou no Chat deve debitar mcoCoins do perfil do usu\u00e1rio conforme o custo da opera\u00e7\u00e3o (ex: 10 mcoCoins por cria\u00e7\u00e3o de texto/c\u00f3digo, 50 mcoCoins por v\u00eddeo/imagem). A execu\u00e7\u00e3o deve falhar com erro 402 se o saldo for insuficiente.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 2. Requisitos N\u00e3o Funcionais (NFR)\\n\\n### NFR-AW-001: Seguran\u00e7a e Isolamento (Multi-tenancy)\\nTodas as queries do banco de dados para os objetos `aios_*` devem possuir a restri\u00e7\u00e3o RLS enforceada de `user_id = auth.uid()`, garantindo que um usu\u00e1rio comum nunca veja dados de outro.\\n\\n### NFR-AW-002: \n<truncated 342 bytes>",

  "CodeContent": "\"# FRD: Functional Requirements Document \u2014 Agentic Workspace\\n\\n**Vers\u00e3o:** 1.0\\n**Data:** 2026-06-06\\n**Projeto:** agentic-workspace\\n**BoK Applied:** SWEBOK v4 (Software Requirements)\\n**Status:** Approved\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 1. Escopo das Funcionalidades (Functional Requirements - FR)\\n\\n### FR-AW-101: CRUD de Canais de Chat\\n- **A\u00e7\u00e3o:** O usu\u00e1rio pode criar, ler, atualizar e excluir (soft-delete) canais de chat.\\n- **Campos:** Nome (slug-friendly), descri\u00e7\u00e3o, flag `is_private` e `project_id`.\\n- **Restri\u00e7\u00e3o:** Exclus\u00e3o de canais padr\u00e3o (ex: `#geral`) \u00e9 proibida.\\n\\n### FR-AW-102: Mensagens e Threads Aninhadas\\n- **A\u00e7\u00e3o:** Postar mensagens no canal ou em uma thread vinculada a uma mensagem pai.\\n- **Entrada:** Texto, links e caminhos de arquivos anexados (m\u00eddias).\\n- **Processamento:** Triggers no Supabase escutam inser\u00e7\u00f5es em `aios_messages`. Se a mensagem marcar um agente (`@engineer`), o sistema cria um job de invoca\u00e7\u00e3o para o respectivo agente no OpenClaw.\\n\\n### FR-AW-103: Opera\u00e7\u00f5es no Kanban Board\\n- **A\u00e7\u00e3o:** Arrastar tarefas entre colunas (To Do \u2192 In Progress \u2192 Done).\\n- **Entrada:** `task_id`, `source_column_id`, `target_column_id`, `new_position`.\\n- **Valida\u00e7\u00e3o:** Mover para \\\"In Progress\\\" consome mcoCoins e invoca a API do agente correspondente de forma ass\u00edncrona. Mover de volta ou falhar reverte (estorno) ou grava o estado de falha na tarefa.\\n\\n### FR-AW-104: CRUD de Administra\u00e7\u00e3o (Super Admin)\\n- **A\u00e7\u00e3o:** Acesso em `/dashboard/admin` para visualizar todos os canais, threads e tarefas criadas de todos os usu\u00e1rios.\\n- **Controle:** Alterar limites de tokens e editar as almas (`SOUL.md`) dos agentes.\\n- **Valida\u00e7\u00e3o:** Bloqueio server-side impede qualquer usu\u00e1rio sem o papel `admin` na tabela `user_roles` de efetuar chamadas a estas rotas administrativas.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 2. Fluxos e Limites de Dados\\n- **Tamanho m\u00e1ximo de mensagem:** 8000 caracteres.\\n- **Limite de Threads por canal:** 500 threads ativas (descarte autom\u00e1tico de threads inativas por mais de 30 dias).\n<truncated 96 bytes>",

  "CodeContent": "\"# Quality Metrics & FMEA: Agentic Workspace\\n\\n**Vers\u00e3o:** 1.0\\n**Data:** 2026-06-06\\n**Projeto:** agentic-workspace\\n**BoK Applied:** CMQ/OE BOK (Quality Management + FMEA)\\n**Status:** Approved\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 1. KPIs de Qualidade (Key Performance Indicators)\\n\\n- **KPI-AW-001:** Lat\u00eancia de recebimento da resposta da IA na Thread: target \u2264 5.0s.\\n- **KPI-AW-002:** Taxa de sucesso de reordena\u00e7\u00e3o no Kanban (zero desvios/erros persistentes): target \u2265 99.9%.\\n- **KPI-AW-003:** Ades\u00e3o do isolamento de Tenancy (RLS): target 100% de cobertura (sem vazamentos de ids).\\n- **KPI-AW-004:** Cobertura de Testes Unit\u00e1rios de Hooks: target \u2265 90%.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 2. An\u00e1lise de Modos de Falha e Efeitos (FMEA)\\n\\n| Modo de Falha | Severidade (S) | Ocorr\u00eancia (O) | Detec\u00e7\u00e3o (D) | RPN | A\u00e7\u00e3o Preventiva / Mitiga\u00e7\u00e3o |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| **FMEA-AW-001:** Falha de rede durante o arraste do Kanban | 6 | 7 | 2 | **84** | Rollback otimista e retorno do cart\u00e3o para a coluna anterior. |\\n| **FMEA-AW-002:** Invoca\u00e7\u00e3o sem mcoCoins (bypass na UI) | 9 | 3 | 1 | **27** | Bloqueio server-side na RPC e rejei\u00e7\u00e3o imediata da transa\u00e7\u00e3o. |\\n| **FMEA-AW-003:** Resposta do agente travada (Timeout no OpenClaw) | 8 | 4 | 3 | **96** | Watchdog em background que marca a mensagem como \\\"timeout\\\" ap\u00f3s 120s e libera o lock do card. |\\n| **FMEA-AW-004:** Tentativa de invas\u00e3o / vazamento de RLS | 10 | 1 | 2 | **20** | Auditoria de seguran\u00e7a automatizada `/security-review` antes de todo deploy. |\\n\\n*RPN (Risk Priority Number) = Severidade (1-10) \u00d7 Ocorr\u00eancia (1-10) \u00d7 Detec\u00e7\u00e3o (1-10)*\\n*A\u00e7\u00f5es recomendadas para RPN > 80: monitoramento em infra_health_logs e testes regressivos dedicados.*\\n\"",

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

**Discovering Key Functionality**

I've just realized the `makeChain` helper in `useKnowledgeMesh.test.ts` is pivotal. Specifically, lines 29-39 present a chainable Supabase query builder that resolves upon being awaited. This could be the exact tool I've been seeking.


**Implementing Mock Supabase**

I'm now building out the mock Supabase client. I have extended `makeChain` to handle `insert` and `update` calls, vital for testing our `create` and `update` functions. The `from` method in the mock Supabase now dynamically returns a chain based on the table name, making it easier to manage the mock data for each table. The current implementation has mocks for `aios_channels`, `aios_threads`, `aios_messages`, `aios_kanban_boards`, `aios_kanban_columns` and `aios_kanban_tasks`.


**Refining Supabase Mocks**

I'm revisiting the mock Supabase's `insert` method. Previously, `insert` just returned the chain, but now I think I need to modify `makeChain`. My latest thought is to customize the resolution of `makeChain` based on the context of the call, particularly for `insert`. Right now the chain resolves with the original `resolveWith` data, but now I think `insert` needs to return the *inserted* object, not the pre-existing data. If `from` initially returns the mock data, then an `insert` should return the newly created record. Otherwise, calls like `.select().single()` will resolve to the original array and not the new object!


**Adapting Test Strategies**

I've realized that overriding the `from` implementation in each test using `mockImplementationOnce` is the cleanest and most flexible approach. This allows complete control over the mocked Supabase client's behavior in each specific scenario. I'm excited by this approach. I have constructed the skeleton of each test for `src/test/agentic-workspace.test.ts`. This involved looking at how the `useKnowledgeMesh.test.ts` implemented the mock, and constructing example tests for fetching and updating channels, messages, kanban boards, and kanban tasks.

</details>

## 🤖 Gemini *(19:57:54)*

Concluí a execução completa do plano de desenvolvimento do **Agentic Workspace** e a integração da nova perspectiva trazida pelo sincronismo da Squad (Jarvis/Slack model). A estrutura agora funciona sob o modelo de **Blackboard Coerente**, onde o banco de dados Supabase é a fonte da verdade relacional determinística que previne o estouro de contexto das LLMs e permite a colaboração assíncrona.

Aqui está o resumo do que foi entregue e validado:

### 1. Suíte BoK (docs/bok/agentic-workspace/)
Criação dos 9 documentos de referência metodológica (MRD, BRD, PRD, FRD, SDD, Data Model, Process Flow, Quality Metrics, Index) sob as regras do *Master Execution Protocol*.

### 2. Infraestrutura e Banco de Dados (Supabase Migration)
A migration [20260606180000_agentic_workspace.sql](file:///home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/supabase/migrations/20260606180000_agentic_workspace.sql) foi aplicada com sucesso no banco de dados remoto via CLI do Supabase. Ela define as tabelas de suporte:
- `aios_channels`, `aios_threads` e `aios_messages` (com coluna `metadata jsonb` para suportar futuras integrações com `Slack_Interface_Connector` e `OpenClaw_NeuralBridge_API`).
- `aios_kanban_boards`, `aios_kanban_columns` e `aios_kanban_tasks` (com a coluna `assigned_agent_id` mapeada para a nossa Squad Pantheon).
- Políticas **RLS ativas e restritas** por `user_id = auth.uid()`.

### 3. Hooks de Conexão e UI (Harness Layer)
